===== root-review-targets.md =====
# Root review leads — preparation only, withheld from original angles

Original 262–264 source/context and full raw fields are saved. All six selected
ACIP/English fields occur in the unchanged physical C05 original, using whitespace
normalization for lookup only. Sixteen exact lexical queries retain thirteen
full entry occurrences and all three empty selections. The original leading
backtick on262 is present physically and falls under registered E-040 structural
section opening; the saved historical record is not a newly measured count.

- 262: the second outline section is the author's own position, unlike the
  preceding opponent's claim. HGM18575 rang lugs bzhag pa explicitly attests
  establish our own position; assess whole compound versus member scope and
  source pronoun licensing. Preserve exact repeated wet/flowing/glass/water,
  three beings, three objects and three parts occurrences.
- 262: gzhi mthun pa (16438) has objects which are both, while source/English
  deny one thing simultaneously three different objects. Preserve the entire
  negative logical relation without relabeling object vocabulary or swallowing
  source negation. tshad mtshungs exact selection is empty, not proof of absence;
  evaluate three identical valid perceptions against local phrase structure.
  gsum ldan (25779) has with three / with three different / with the three;
  cha shas (4384) has parts/part/distinct parts. Full source roles determine
  compound boundaries and potential tighter members.
- 263: two grub occurrences and their negated temporal argument need distinct
  owners (first started/came). The ma zhig ... bar limit is rendered until it
  eventually ends; avoid mapping ma as a separate English not in that limit.
  gnas pa (10314) explicitly includes stay, and the outer negative scope remains.
- 263–264: nyer len (5840) attests material cause, lhan cig byed rkyen (21656)
  contributing factor, and brten nas (7086) based on. Distinguish the material
  object part from karma as the contributing condition; supplied both/of-these
  framing must have a defensible whole-relation source span if retained.
- 263–264: rigs 'dra phyi ma (25288) explicitly includes later continuation;
  rigs 'dra (18898) and phyi ma (11877) provide possible tighter members.
  skye ba (703) includes starts being. 263's source rnag khrag maps the reversed
  English blood and pus; assign any members to their actual English occurrences.
  264's repeated glass/water explanatory phrases cannot create extra Tibetan
  word occurrences. Bound chur keeps its source morphology.
- All repetitions, crossing order, noun roles and causal clauses must be checked
  against full original rows. No glossary string alone determines phrase depth
  or permits supplied English. Uncertainty/distributed text stays omitted with
  reasons, never falsely declared null. All work remains PROVISIONAL.

The source's one skye ba is linked to the first occurs; the English reprise it occurs remains unwrapped. cig car du owns together; sit down is expansion. gzhi mthun pa is omitted because its common-locus relation is distributed through one thing which is simultaneously and its complements; it is not a null. nges par is omitted because its modal force is distributed with dgos in necessary. own position excludes supplied our. ===== tibetan/263/report.md =====
C05:263 — independent Tibetan-first proposal by Codex; PROVISIONAL, no semantic approval.
Challenge first: temporal ma ... bar du is rendered until ... ends, so that ma is null with an explicit grammatical-fusion reason; matrix ma separately owns not.
PROVISIONAL machine alignment by Codex, Tibetan-first proposal; no human semantic approval. The second cha shas gsum is resumed as they; its lexical correspondence is distributed and omitted, not null. The temporal ma before zhig is fused into until ... ends, distinct from the matrix ma linked to not. tsam and de/de'i resumptions are omitted where English expands or redistributes them.
